# Household hh_s17 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: balcony, bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, entry, kitchen, living, office.

Residents:

- **Dana** (resident_1): a student with classes out; very tidy (tidiness 0.76); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.40); sometimes forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.44); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the office desk. Hobbies: movies, napping, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.
- **Sam** (resident_2): retired; very tidy (tidiness 0.84); erratic timing (jitter scale 1.27); often forgets pocket items; mood moderately affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.46); sleeps in bedroom_2, works at the bedroom_2 desk. Hobbies: board_games, friends, running. Chores they take on: fixing, grocery_run.

**Active causes**

## Day 14 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d14`: Sam hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (15:25–17:18).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d14`: Dana is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:05–15:35).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Dana): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Sam): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Dana has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Sam has low energy (0.04): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

## Day 25 — Friday

**Active causes**

- `ep:long_call_with_a_friend:resident_2:d25`: Sam gets a long call from a friend and takes it on the couch; the phone is left there afterwards (19:17–20:02).
- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_1:d25`: Dana is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (14:32–17:43).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_2:d25`: Sam has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_1:d25`: Dana is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (13:53–15:53).
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Dana has low energy (0.12): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

Internal states (0–1): Dana energy 0.12, hurriedness 0.47, distraction 0.42; Sam energy 0.30, hurriedness 0.15, distraction 0.21
